Description
[
]
Bloodrend
is an
Attunement
that grants the user the ability to control, manipulate, and conjure their own and other's
blood
.
Bloodrenders excel in long fights, their power ramping up as the fight goes on due to the effects applied to enemies from Blood Poison and Blood Drain. They can counter their opponents' sustain while using sustain to benefit themselves. However, Bloodrend is difficult to make use of outside of single target combat, as blood poison decays, making it hard to achieve high percentages of it against multiple enemies.
It's extremely effective at long to mid range. To counter this attunement, prioritize staying up close and applying constant pressure, as Bloodrend lacks close range low windup mantras. Keep in mind that any mistake is likely to lead to you getting severely punished by the Bloodrend user, even at close range from Mantras like
Bloodcurdle
.
PvE Mechanic(s)
[
]
Bloodrenders can leverage the
Hemorrhaging
mechanic, dealing a percentage, scaling on Bloodrend investment and player level, of a PvE enemy's current health in a singular damage instance, once a threshold of blood loss is met. At Power 20 with 100 Bloodrend, this deals 17.5% of the target's current health. This threshold can be built up by using any source of blood loss, not exclusively Bloodrend, and does not explicitly require the player to be a Bloodrender to use. When this procs, the enemy will lose a percentage of the
Blood Poisoning
you've applied to them.
Obtainment
[
]
The
Attunement Trainer
,
Vyrelda
, can be located in an igloo on the east side of Firfire Village, caring for an injured diver. She is only visible after having killed
Chaser
at least once. After killing Chaser, re-enter Layer 2 and go talk with Vyrelda - she will talk about her desertion from
The Ministry
, and how she evaded the Ministry for so long, who are searching for her. She mentions "Bloodrend" and how she only wished to be taught under the Second Prophet for purely

Overview
[
]
Bosses
in
Deepwoken
are powerful
Enemies
and
Monsters
that possess heavy backstories and unique abilities. They award Resonance
Progress
,
Knowledge
, and unique
weapons
such as the
Purple Cloud
,
Dissonant Chimecaller
, and the
Frostthorn
.
Bosses
[
]
The concrete, in-game method of determining if an Enemy/Monster is a
Boss
is whether or not they can be used to obtain
Chain of Perfection
. However, every Boss has most, if not all of the secondary criteria below.
Unique Summoning System or Guaranteed Spawn Locations

upon defeat.

upon defeat.

upon defeat.

upon defeat.

Character Stats
[
]
Each player gets 330 total investment points per character, from Level 1-20, including investment points given to them by their character’s aspect (+3 and +2 stats).
Health
[
]
The player's max Health Points. Starting value is 220 (+4 at Power 1).
Current HP is tracked on the health bar in %, from 100 to 0. When it reaches 0%, caused by taking damage, the player becomes
Unconscious
.
How to increase:
Power
(+4 Per power),
Equipment
,
Fortitude
(+0.5 Per investment (this is halved to +0.25 per investment past 50 Fortitude)),
Talents
,
Oaths
,
Vitality
(+10 per point; max +60 at 6 Vitality)
Posture
[
]
The player's max Posture. Starting value is 20.
Current Posture is tracked on the posture bar in %, from 0 to 100. When it reaches 100%, reached by blocking too many hits, the player is guard broken; they are stunned and their posture is cleared.
Max Posture can be greatly increased by using a
Shield
, though at the cost of reducing the amount of posture restored on parry by 40%.
How to increase:
Shields
,
Equipment
,
Talents
Ether
[
]
The player's max Ether. Starting value is 120.
Current Ether is tracked on the ether bar in %, from 0 to 100. Among other things, using Mantras depletes your Ether bar.
When in combat, the users Ether regeneration scales on their current
Tempo
.
How to increase:
Intelligence
,
Charisma
,
Equipment
,
Talents
Tempo
[
]
The player's max Tempo. Starting value is 120.
Current Tempo is tracked on the Tempo bar in %, from 0 to 100. Tempo is primarily used when
Venting
.
The Tempo bar affects your Ether regeneration while in combat.
Vents cost 40 Tempo to use.
How to increase:
Erudition
(+5 Tempo and +5% Tempo Regen per point; max +30 and +30% at 6 Erudition), some
Equipment
/
Outfit
Talents
Sanity
[
]
The player's max Sanity. Starting value is 80.
Insanity is tracked with Sanity as its max value. When your sanity % goes below 70%, detrimental effects will be applied, with additional effects being added the lower your Sanity gets. (Check the
Insanity
page for more info)
How to increase:
Willpower
,
Equipment
,
Talents
The
Ganymede
aspect grants a 1.2x Sanity gain multiplier.
Stealth
[
]
The player's max Stealth. Starting value is 0%.
Player becomes stealthier, lowering the detection range of mobs.
How to increase:
Agility
,
Equipment
,
Talents
(check the
Stealth
page for more info)
The
Felinor
aspect grants 20% starting Stealth, and has a 1.2x Stealth gain multiplier.
Carry Load
[
]
The player's maximum inventory capacity. Starting value is 100.
Current inventory usage and maximum capacity can be tracked at the bottom of the inventory. Player can carry more item

Navigation
Description
[
]
Flamecharm
is an
Attunement
that grants the user the ability to create flames using Ether.. These abilities are primarily focused on DOT and AoE effects, while still providing a notable amount of utility and mobility.
This Attunement is ideal for new players, due to its innate ability to grip knocked humanoid NPCs with its DOT burn effect, alongside its superior utility in comparison with other Attunements, making progression and exploration much easier.
As mentioned previously, Flamecharm focuses on DOT and AoE effects. This Attunement's mantras usually create a large hitbox either around the user or in front of them, making hitting your opponent incredibly easy at close range. To compensate, its mantras rarely propel the user horizontally, with most of its mantras either slowing the user down or preventing movement almost entirely. This makes builds with lots of movement options and range viable against this Attunement, as it limits the amount of mantras you have to worry about. Do note that it does have a couple of options in order to close the distance or deal damage at considerable range, namely Fire Palm, Flaming Scourge modified with Reversal Spark, and the Flame Dancer talents.
Obtainment
[
]
The
Attunement Trainer
,
Amashi
, can be located at the
Songseeker Temple
in
Songseeker Wilds
. Once you give Amashi two Dying Embers, he will hand you the
Hearthblood Potion
which you must drink in order to obtain the Attunement. Alternatively, you can simply pick the Attunement from Character Creation.
Training
[
]
Flamecharm can be trained by using the
Hearthgem
to increase the attribute after either landing Flamecharm Mantras or eating
Dying Embers
to gain enough EXP.
Along with all other elements, users can see their Mantras becoming stronger with practice:
At

==================================================

Pathfinder
Exclusive
This page describes content that is exclusive to the
Pathfinder
gamemode.
This content is not accessible outside of this gamemode.
“ YOU ARE IMBALANCED. LIKE A WALKING CORPSE LISTING TO ITS SIDE. YOUR FRAGMENTS WILL BE LINED IN A ROW. LIKE TIN SOLDIERS. ALL THE SAME. ”
― Shrine of Order
If you're looking for another shrine, return to
Deep Shrines
or head to
Navigation
.
Location
[
]
Located in:
Songseeker Mines
, through the passage containing mudskippers.
Also located in
Temple of Hearts
, being the first large shrine on the left when going towards the
Shrine of Yun'Shul
.
Songseeker Mines entrance
Guide
Effects/Details
[
]
Effects
[
]
For 10
, average your stats (up to a limit*). This can only be done once per character.
You need Power 8+ and no
Oath
(having
Oathless
will still allow you) to perform this deal.
If you took an Oath beforehand, you may break the Oath to order yourself. This has
consequences
of its own, however.
Details
[
]
Any core attribute or weapon stat
cannot lose more than 25 investment points
from Shrine of Order. Attunement stats can lose any amount of investment points.
The effectiveness of some Talents may be reduced if you do not meet their requirements.
This does not apply to all Talents, see info of specific Talents on the
Talents
page.
The main purpose of the Shrine of Order is allowing you to obtain expensive
Talents
or
Mantras
, to then take a portion of these points away to invest into other stats, allowing your builds to effectively have more points than intended.
Only the stats you have invested at least 1 point into will be ordered by Shrine of Order. In the case of racial points, you need to invest 1 point manually into the stat (making it 3 or 4) for it to count.
Any points that cannot be evenly distributed during the averaging process will be returned to you as free points.
Adept/Expert/Master Attunement and Attunement Unbound Talents disappear after the attribute goes below their requirements.
Mantras' upgrade levels will be downgraded to the appropriate level if the requirements for their current levels are no longer met.
If you fall below an Equipment or Weapon's attribute requirements through Shrine of Order, that item will be automatically unequipped.
If you no longer meet the requirements of your
Outfit
after using Shrine of Order, it will stay equipped and you will be able to unequip/reequip it at any time as Outfit stat requirements are only enforced when crafting them.
Does not progress towards the
Contractor Oath
obtainment.
If needed, there are a few ways to get around Shrine of Order's Power 8 requirement.
You can use
Shrine of Blasphemy
at Powers 5-7 to level up three times and Shrine of Order with the stats of a Power 5-7.
At Power 7, you can invest 3 points to reach Power 8 then use
Shrine of Mastery
to remove the points, allowing you to Shrine of Order with slightly below the stats of a normal Power 8. Admittedly this has very niche use cases.

### 📖 [하위 연관 위키: Deep Shrines]
Not to be confused with
Shrine of Courage
,
Shrine of the Drowned
,
Shrine of Yun'Shul
, or
Shrines
(aka Fountain Gates).
Pathfinder
Exclusive
This page describes content that is exclusive to the
Pathfinder
gamemode.
This content is not accessible outside of this gamemode.
Contents
1
Description
1.1

2

Description
[
]
Deep Shrines
are a group of 10 shrines sharing identical appearance (excluding Solitude), scattered across the Lumen. Each bestows different deals in exchange for the client's
knowledge
. Each Deep Shrine can be found in a unique location in the overworld as well as the
Temple of Hearts
, accessible by Deepbounds and anyone who has obtained their resonance.
Limitations
[
]
Deep Shrines
can only be interacted with after approximately ~10 minutes of spawning, so trying to Deepbound won't work.
Shrines
[
]
Shrine of Temptation
Shrine of Blasphemy
Shrine of Temptation
:
Trade a Mantra of your choice and 5
to gain a Whisper Draw.
Shrine of Blasphemy
:
For 5
,
POWER 1-16
instantly Power Up 3 times, gaining the relevant amount of Talents and Whisper Draws, granting you a
Flaw
, Or
POWER 17-20
reroll a Flaw of your choice into another, random base
Flaw
.
Shrine of Mastery
Shrine of Chance
Shrine of Mastery
:
For 1
, trade 1 of your Trait Points into another Trait. You can also remove attribute points.
Shrine of Chance
:
For 5
, trade a Talent of your choice to obtain a Talent hand of equal rarity (e.g. Common/Advanced = Common/Advanced, Rare = Rare) OR a Rare Talent for a Talent hand of only Common/Advanced Talents. For 10
, trade a Common/Advanced Talent of your choice for a Talent hand consisting only of Rare Talents.
Shrine of Order
Shrine of Conceit
Shrine of Order
:
For 10
, average your stats (up to a limit of -25 points on main stats, with no limit on attunement stats). Can only be done once per character. You must need
POWER 8+ AND
NO OATH
to use this.
Shrine of Conceit
:
For 1
, unburn a card of your choice. Note that the Ace is
NOT REFUNDED
.
Shrine of Division
Shrine of Unification
Shrine of Division:
For 5
and a Mantra of your choice, obtain a Roll 2.
Shrine of Unification
:
For 5
and 2 Talents of your choice, get a Whisper Draw.
Gallery
[
]
Concept art
Trivia
[
]
Ganymedes
can do deep deals with a discount of 1 knowledge off of all shrines, except the Shrine of Conceit and the Shrine of Mastery.
Deep Shrines, most notably the Shrine of Chance and the Shrine of Order, are obvious references to the roguelite Risk of Rain 2, sharing their names with and functioning similarly to the Shrine of Chance and Shrine of Order from that game.
In Risk of Rain 2, the Shrine of Chance takes money in exchange for a chance to gain an item (cannot be used after succeeding twice, cost increases each attempt), and the Shrine of Order randomly selects one item from each rarity and converts all other items of the same rarity to the selected item.
Ironically, in that game, Shrine of Order was more or

### 📖 [하위 연관 위키: Deep Shrines/Shrine of Blasphemy]
Pathfinder
Exclusive
This page describes content that is exclusive to the
Pathfinder
gamemode.
This content is not accessible outside of this gamemode.
“ YOU ARE WEAK. FRAGILE. I WILL SHAPE YOUR SKULL INTO SOMETHING MAGNIFICENT. ”
― Shrine of Blasphemy
If you're looking for another shrine return to
Deep Shrines
or head to
Navigation
.
Description
[
]
Located deep within
The Hidden Village
of
Erisia
, obscured by vines and wood.
Effects/Details
[
]
Effects
[
]
This shrine has two effects depending on your
Power
:
Power 1-16: For 5
, instantly Power Up
three
times, gaining the relevant amount of
Talents
and
Whisper Draws
. However, you gain a
Flaw
and must catch up on the 45 Investment points of these three Power Ups.
This can only be done once per character.
Power 17-20: For 5
, reroll a
Flaw
of your choice into a random base Flaw.
This deal can also be done at any Power if you've already used the previous deal to force three Power ups.
Details
[
]
In the early game, this shrine is a must for early
Shrine of Order
builds (which do not get to Power 8 pre-shrine). Otherwise, it is advised you do not Shrine of Blasphemy to Power Up three times, as the extra
Flaw
gained and
Knowledge
consumed may not be worth it.
Bear in mind that the many card draws you will perform are based on your current stats.
You do not gain any attribute points from this shrine.
The 45 Investment Points you need to catch up will make it harder to recover a Life while in the Overworld, especially if you don't use free points from the
Adret
Aspect or the Autodidact Boon.
With the three
Power
Ups granted, you immediately gain 3
.
Since you gain bonus damage in PvE based on your level, you immediately attain higher damage against non-players.
Shrine of Blasphemy cannot grant the
Simple
or
Vegetarian
Flaws, any unlockable Flaw (
Blind
or
Fugitive
), and cannot grant nor remove any special Flaws (like
Tarnished
or
Disgraced
).
Rerolling
one
Flaw
worth two Flaw points (
Simple
,
Fugitive
) results in
two
Flaws (each worth one Flaw point).
[requires testing]

### 📖 [하위 연관 위키: Deep Shrines/Shrine of Chance]
Pathfinder
Exclusive
This page describes content that is exclusive to the
Pathfinder
gamemode.
This content is not accessible outside of this gamemode.
“ PUPPET. WHAT DOES YOUR HEART DESIRE? ”
― Shrine of Chance
If you're looking for another shrine return to
Deep Shrines
or head to
Navigation
.
Description
[
]
Located behind a breakable wall in the
Meteor Isle
cave (with the crashed meteor), this Deep Shrine allows you to exchange Talents you own for a hand of three semi-random Talents at the cost of Knowledge. The hand of Talents received isn't fully random, as it can be influenced through
Favouring
and
Fortelling
cards, on top of being limited to a specific Talent rarity, as dictated by the specific deal you struck.
Purchasing the
Choice of Chance
Echo Unlock for 200 Echoes permanently increases the amount of cards you receive in a Shrine of Chance hand by one; from three to four. While not necessary, this Echo Unlock is a great quality of life improvement.
When using this Shrine, please make sure to
check if you burned the card you're trying to get
using the
Shrine of Conceit
or
check if you already happen to have it
. Otherwise, it could result in a mistake worth upwards of 10 Knowledge before you realize it wasn't just bad luck or a missing prerequisite.
As with the other Deep Shrines, this is also located in the Temple of Hearts. It has an additional location in the First Layer, inside one of the brown topped ruins found throughout the map.
The entrance to the shrine in the cave
The Shrine of Chance in
The Depths
Shrine of Chance at
Meteor Isle
Effects/Details
[
]
Shrine of Chance
5
Knowledge
Talent Exchange
Exchange a Talent for a hand of Talents of equivalent rarity.
5
Knowledge
Talent Degradation
Exchange a
Rare
Talent for a hand of Common Talents.
10
Knowledge
Talent Elevation
Exchange a Common Talent for a hand of
Rare
Talents.
5
Knowledge
Faction Exchange
Exchange a Faction Talent for a hand of Faction Talents.
Details
[
]
Advanced
and Common Talents share a rarity as far as Shrine of Chance is concerned;
You can exchange a Common Talent for an
Advanced
Talent and vice versa via
Talent Exchange
.
You can exchange a Common or
Advanced
Talent for a
Rare
via
Talent Elevation
.
You can exchange a
Rare
Talent and potentially receive a Talent hand that has both Common and
Advanced
Talents via
Talent Degradation
.
Players who are exiled from their faction are unable to reroll their faction Talents.
When using the
Faction
Talent exchange, you cannot exchange a base Faction Talent to gain a Talent that is exclusive to a
Division
or
Path
. For example, you cannot get a Talent from your
Ensign Division
by exchanging
Crossguard
.
The Talent Draw is affected by Talent Favoring and Foretelling, make sure to use them to not waste Knowledge.
You must meet the requirements at least one non-mutually exclusive Talent in order to exchange a Talent for another.
For example, if the only
Rare
Talents you can obtain are
Observation
and
Safety Dance
, you wil

### 📖 [하위 연관 위키: Deep Shrines/Shrine of Mastery]
Pathfinder
Exclusive
This page describes content that is exclusive to the
Pathfinder
gamemode.
This content is not accessible outside of this gamemode.
“ CHOOSE THE TRAIT OF YOUR SOUL TO DIMINISH. ”
― Shrine of Mastery
If you're looking for another shrine, return to
Deep Shrines
or head to
Navigation
.
Description
[
]
Located in the entrance cave before
Deep Widow
in the
Widow's Hollow
, in the little house where
Ciea
is located.
Effects/Details
[
]
Effects
[
]
Shrine of Mastery has two different effects.
For 1
, you can trade 1 of your
Trait
Points into another Trait.
For 1
, you can remove 1 of your invested attribute points, allowing you to reinvest it. A maximum of 3 points can be removed per character.
Details
[
]
You cannot add more than 6 Points into a given Trait.
You can only remove 3 attribute points per character.
You cannot remove innate attribute points granted by your aspect.
This limitation will be removed if you have the
Multifaceted
Echo Unlock, as it converts innate attribute points into regular investment points.
You cannot reduce an attribute if you have Talent(s) that require equal to or higher investment on that attribute.
For example: If you went 40 Strength to pick
Showstopper
, you wouldn't be able to remove points from Strength to go below 40 investment, as long as you have Showstopper. This also applies if your Strength was already below 40 due to
Shrine of Order
usage, entirely preventing you from reducing the attribute.
This applies to the Attunement mastery Talents as well, such as Adept Flamecharmer; Shrine of Mastery will not allow you to go below the 1/20/30/50 Attunement investment milestones, in most scenarios (see
below
).
This restriction does not apply to the following Talent requirement types, allowing you to freely uninvest:
The alternative attribute requirements for Talents; for example, as
Potion Quaffer
requires 15 Intelligence and [15 Fortitude or 15 Willpower], you can reduce your Fortitude and Willpower investment, but you cannot reduce your Intelligence investment below Potion Quaffer's requirements.
Category attributes on Talents that require attribute category investment (
Mind
,
Body
, Element, and Weapon). For example, if you have
Grounding Bolt
(90 Thundercall, 90 Weapon), you can reduce your Weapon investment, but not your Thundercall investment.
Attributes Unbound
Talents, though Attunement Unbounded Talents will be removed from your character if you do not meet their requirements.
Attribute requirements for Oath Talents; You can reduce your attributes below your Oath's requirements.
The only exception to this is
Arcwarder
in most scenarios, as you cannot lower your Attunement stats due to the Attunement Adept Talents. This however, can be avoided by using
Shrine of Order
to go
up
to exactly 20 Flamecharm and/or 20 Thundercall, obtaining the Oath, and then going to Shrine of Mastery. Keep in mind that if you invest any attribute points, die (including resetting your character), or leave the ga

Title: Deepwoken Wiki: Oath: Silentheart
URL: https://deepwoken.fandom.com/wiki/Oath:_Silentheart
Description: Official Fandom Wiki document for Oath: Silentheart

==================================================

For the version that existed prior to the 13th September, 2024, see
here
.
“ Your old heart is no more, hateful one. We have exchanged it for one aligned with our ideals, deafened to the chaos of the Song. You are Silent now, hateful one. ”
― The Dreadstar

Navigation
Description
[
]
Oath
: Silentheart
is one of the 17 obtainable Oaths
.
It sacrifices
Mantras
and
Attunements
as a whole in exchange for the ability to change weapons during battle and a unique set of attacks integrated into your moveset.
“ You feel as though a part of yourself has drifted away, into the vast nothingness of the Song. As sudden as this sensation arrived, a new piece of you took place. This heart beats differently, it drums to the beat of the world, not the Song, ever silent. ”
― Obtaining Silentheart
Silentheart is a result of a Vow made with
The Dreadstar
, a mysterious entity residing in the
Voidsea
made from the souls of various deceased individuals. Vengeful due to its death at the hands of the users of Mantras, it falsely believes it can rewire the hearts of those willing to engage in a Vow with it to avoid using the Song. Upon the Silentheart reaching their true potential, the Dreadstar fuses with them to form a single entity.
Unfortunately, the Song is so permeating that the Silentheart is only able to sidestep the Mantras that killed them; their newfound abilities too rely on the Song. Had it truly become disconnected, the Silentheart would have become an ordinary human being with little strength or durability, akin to a
Lightkeeper
and their inability to use the Song.
The Vow is one of malevolent nature however, as The Dreadstar likely gave the user's previous, song-attuned heart to the Drowned God
Yun'Shul, Keeper of Hearts
, possibly in an attempt to earn their favour. This is evidenced by The Dreadstar themselves, who state that the user's "Heart has already been claimed" upon
Oathbreak
.
Obtainment
[
]
Meet the following requirements in any order:
Be attunementless
Have a total of 75 points in Weapons (LHT, MED, HVY all count, you can do 25/25/25, for example)
Have 25 points in Strength
Have 25 points in
either
Charisma or Agility
Die to a
player
that uses an attunement. Chime of Conflict does not count, nor does getting gripped in the
Second Layer
with a Light Hook.
Encounter a ghost npc on the
Beloved Zofia
and speak to the Dreadstar afterwards, if you are the first player to do so on a server this will spawn the dread star in, if you however are not the first in the server to do so you must slay/survive a ghost encounter to interact with the Dreadstar.
Once
all
met, head over to the
Beloved Zofia
in the
Voidsea
to talk to The Dreadstar
Failing to meet any of the first 4 will cause The Dreadstar to say you do not have aptitude.
If you haven't died to an attunement player, The Dreadstar will say "You lack focus on your rage".
For a more in-depth walkthrough, consider watching
this video
.
(While the video suggests starting
Suyuan
's quest, intending that it spawns the ship - obtaining the Oath doesn't actually depend on it.)
Progression
[
]
This Oath is progressed via the killing of
Attunement Trainers
(excluding
Regek
, and
Beatra
).
This can be done in any order; however, the recommended order is
Nell
,
Friid
,
Amashi
,
Vyrelda
,
Funke
,
Adar
, and
Nostor
due to their locations.
Note that killing the Spelltrainers
will make you lose Reputation
with some factions :
Nell and Friid with Etrea
Funke with Summer Company
Adar with Ignition Union
Alternatively, if you've fully progressed the Oath previously, 10
Knowledge
can be spent to immediately unlock all related Talents.
Silentheart
cannot
be reobtained if you
Oathbreak
it, as the Dreadstar will not spawn.
Effects
[
]
Ability to wield weapons with 25 investment points lower than its weapon stat requirement (this will not work on core attribute requirements).
Entirely lose access to using Mantras
; learned Mantras can still be viewed in your Journal and turned in to
Shrine of Division
, but they will disappear from your inventory and they cannot be used.
Attunements are unable to be obtained.
"Elemental" weapons
cannot
be equipped.
All Silentheart abilities gain a secondary damage instance in PvE that deals 133% of the original attack's damage. This benefits from
Chain of Perfection
twice.
You gain glowing red tattoos coursing over your arms and torso.
Your eye color becomes duller and darker.
Eye color difference
Silentheart Visual
Abilities
[
]
Talents
[
]
Dread Fighter
- Parrying and getting hit by mantras now give you stacks of
Dread
. Each stack lasts for 15 seconds.
Dread stacks grant 5% Mantra resistance per stack, stacking up to 25% with 5 stacks.
Stacks of dread are represented as a halo that grows from your chest. Each stack makes the halo larger and raises your mantra resistance.
Maxed Dread Stacks
Merciless Blade
- Increases your Execute speed by 40%.
Reduces execution animation duration by 1.2 seconds (3 seconds down to 1.8).
Comes with the Oath.
True Vantage
-
Venting
makes you invisible briefly, as well as boosting your speed.
This turns your Vents Red which is purely cosmetic.
If you have used Dyes, instead turns your vents to your tattoo colour.
This does not affect
Brute Strength
's Vent.
Unmatched Dexterity
- Press
X
to Quick Swap between weapons within your toolbar.
Swaps your current weapon with a random one on your hotbar.
Weapon swap will not swap to a weapon you are currently holding (not equipped, but holding the weapon out).
You can use this to confirm a weapon swap while using 3 weapons.
This Talent passively allows Silenthearts to manually swap equipment or weapons while in danger as well.
Notably, the
Curse of the No Life King
and
Curse of Void
curse effects of always having a stained healthbar have their durations extended for 60 seconds upon taking damage. This means you cannot avoid being sent to
The Depths
by swapping to a non-cursed weapon just before being knocked Unconscious.
Talent Abilities
[
]
Flow State
- Enter a moment of extreme focus in which you can change the windup of your special attacks with the correct timing.
Activating Ankle Cutter, Mayhem, Relentless Hunt, or Rising Star within 2 seconds of activating Flow State will enhance these attacks.
Flow State has a 10 second cooldown, landing a Flow State-enhanced Silentheart ability will reset Flow State's cooldown.
The Flow State enhanced effects on Silentheart abilities will be highlighted in
red
.
Flow state is a Talent tool.
Flow State changes the behavior of a move:
Increases the damage of most Silentheart abilities.
Grants Mayhem the ability to autogrip.
May add additional effects to certain Silentheart abilities.
Shortens the windup of most Silentheart abilities.
Adds a loud whooshing sound effect right before you do the move.
Flow State activated
Ankle Cutter
- You can now do a special ground slash while sliding, applying a temporary speed debuff to those hit, as well as preventing jumping.
Deals
37.5/
52.5
damage with innate
Bleed
.
Has a fixed attack speed across all weapon types.
Can be used while sliding down slopes at no cost to your speed.
Flow State shortens the windup and increases the damage.
5 second cooldown.
Standard Windup
Mayhem
- Initiate a special dash attack by pressing M1 during the start of your dodge.
16s CD per weapon type, 6s CD if feinted.
Can be feinted by pressing
M2
during its windup.
Movesets differ depending on the weapon type. Cooldowns are per weapon type; using Light and switching to Heavy will allow you to immediately use the Heavy variant.
All variants are capable of
auto-gripping
with Flow State active.

- deals
7.5
Oath damage with 40 Light Weapon scaling, dealing
37.5

+2.5

- deals

- deals

+7.5
damage.

- M1 while doing an Aerial Attack to initiate a special gap closer attack.
20s CD per weapon type, 6s CD if feinted.
Can be feinted by pressing

during its windup.
Mostly used as a gap closer.

Notes
[
]
If you have 0% Tempo, your Rising Star, Relentless Hunt, and Mayhem deal reduced damage, posture damage, and will have longer windups.
Heavy: -15 damage, -5 posture damage / Medium: -15 damage, -4 posture damage / Light: -7.5 damage, -1 posture damage.
All Silentheart abilities count as weapon damage, proccing
Talents
and
Enchantments
. However, Silentheart has a 30 second cooldown on a Silentheart Ability proccing an Enchantment.
Using any Silentheart ability will remove your active Whistleguard, granted you are wearing a
Whistling Periapt
.
Dying to an attunement user does not necessarily mean being manually executed by them; dying by any method as long as they are the killer will work.
You will still gain
Whisper Draws
when leveling up, and from Special Mantra Hands.
Wyrmtooth
can utilize both the Medium and Heavy Silentheart abilities, using Heavy if the Medium ones are on cooldown.
The Attunement-less trainer, Regek, cannot be killed for progression despite Physical Mantras also using the song. This may be due to the fact they do not "misuse" the song like the other attunement trainers, and therefore it isn't counted as "song abuse" by The Dreadstar.
Beatra
cannot be killed for progression due to her old age.
Silentheart's vow rejects the Words of the Song (Mantra chants). Resonances and Murmurs are not words and thus, are not affected by this Oath.
You will be able to pass
Aelita
's game by using any of your Silentheart abilities.
Funnily enough, before an unknown update, Silenthearts were unable to do so.
Vyrelda
will still appear in the Derelict Highchurch regardless of if you've achieved bloodrend on the account so long as you have the oath
To make the Dreadstar appear, interact with one of the Ghosts on the ship and fight them.
During a brief moment before the weekly update Silentheart was reworked in, you were capable of receiving a bugged version of the
Unmatched Dexterity
Talent card with a different description alongside the old Talent set. (image attachment)
Silent Cascade
,
Rising Star
, and
normal uppercut
all share same keybinds, however there's a clear priority (the further to the left = the more prioritized)
Silent Cascade > Rising Star > Uppercut
Trivia
[
]
The lines that adorn a Silentheart's arms and chest are tattoos, not veins.
Despite this, they will lose their coloration should the Silentheart be killed by
Blood Scourge
, which specifically drains blood.
The dialogue with the
Attunement Trainers
changes after obtaining Silentheart, stating "Your Ether ... Just what have you done?"
Vyrelda
has further unique dialogue, stating "I don't have time for this."
Previously, there was a bug allowing you to acquire an Attunement and its Talents (albeit not its Mantras) by drinking an
Attunement Potion
while in the Dreadstar's dialogue. However, this was eventually fixed. This let some people have Talents like
Flaming Flourish
along with Silentheart, allowing for huge damage potential. Builds created before it was patched still have their abilities.
On top of changing the color of your Silentheart tattoos,
dying
the Silentheart Oath cosmetic will also recolor your True Vantage Vent, Dread Fighter, and Flow State.
The sound of landing a hit / obtaining a stack of dread is the same sound used in Terraria when a Fallen Star lands.

1
]
Ankle Cutter is a reference to Ankle Cutter from
Black Desert Online.

Fire Emblem
series.

weapon of the same name
in
Fire Emblem Echoes: Shadows of Valentia
.
Both
Dread Fighter
and
Lodestar
reference obtainable classes in different
Fire Emblem
games.
True Vantage is a reference to the skill
Vantage
i

Description
[
]
Oath
: Oathless
is one of the 17 obtainable Oaths in
Deepwoken.
It is a rather varied Oath, given that its purpose is to extend the capabilities of what would be considered a Pathfinder. Together with
Linkstrider
,
Fadetrimmer
,
Dawnwalker
, and
Contractor
, it is one of 5 Oaths that have no direct stat requirements.
Despite its oxymoronic nature, this still counts as an Oath. So other Oaths like
Linkstrider
or
Contractor
that has their progression tied to killing Oath users can progress by killing people with this Oath, along with other Oaths.
Obtainment
[
]
To start the Oathless quest, head to the docks at
Miner's Landing
and pass through the entrance. Climb up the ramp in front of
Tolkat
walking onwards until you find a log by the ledge facing the sea highway. Through this log is a cave in which
Cerulean
can be found leaning against a wall. They'll inform you that, in order to understand the freedom of being Oathless, you must seek out their disciples.
If you have obtained Oathless, you may speak to
Cerulean
again to permanently unlock it. This skips the quest on all of your characters/slots, even if that character/slot was created before you permanently unlocked Oathless.
[Note that obtaining Oathless via any method requires 10 minutes of playtime on that character.]
Oathless
can
be reobtained if you
Oathbreak
it.
Aforementioned log tunnel
Though order does not matter, the following order is recommended.
Viridian
is located at
Fort Merit
, in the street near the elevator.
Saffron
is located at
Meteor Isle
, in a 2-story house.
Celadon
is located at
Summer Isle
, on the second floor of the guildhall.
After talking to all three of their disciples, return to Cerulean to receive the Oath.
For a more in-depth walkthrough, consider watching
this video
.
Effects
[
]
This Oath grants the player three extra
Wildcard Mantra
slots, 10 Health, 20% Monster Armor, 20% Oath armor, and 5% general PEN. Oath Armor decreases the damage received from the Oath damage type
[
1
]
.
As for its appearance,
nothing
is added to your outfit, making it impossible to tell if a person has Oathless or not, which makes sense because Oathless makes you unbound to an Oath.
Abilities
[
]
Passives
[
]
Oath Armor
- Oathless grants 20% Oath Armor
.
This does not work on every Oath ability, as not all of them deal Oath damage in general.
Due to this being "
armor
" instead of "
resistance
", PEN will affect it and it will be tied to your armor value, being deactivated if your armor is broken.
Oath attacks with multiple damage types will effectively bypass your Oath armor because the game will take your highest applicable armor resistance and apply that for damage calculation.
For example, if you have under 20%

Description

Oath
: Arcwarder
is one of the 17 obtainable Oaths in
Deepwoken.
It heavily focuses on defense; four of its passives are defensive, and the other two are mobility. It still allows for a fair bit of offensive play however as dash allows the user to close gaps and maneuver around opponents, and both combat mantras are as effective if not more in aggressive play.
“ Unlike the Central Authority, the Union's identity is not defined wholly by internal hierarchy, lingering military traditions, and loyalty to power. Instead, it is rooted in the solidarity of toil - a sense of kinship among those working for the same goal. ”
― The Hammer's Call
The Arc Suit is a defence orientated piece of semi-sentient armour that
The Ignition Union
bestows upon their most trusted. It fuses with the soul of those who swear the oath, allowing them to manifest it around them at will. Arcwarders are sworn to become a conduit of protection to their fellow Union mates and do whatever it takes to defend them. Notably, Arcwarders are the only people known to utilize Plasma in their attacks, likely from a result of the
Flamecharm
and
Thundercall
being combined within the suit.

and Complete Alpha's quest.

to perish due to the parasites. Epsi will always be outside of
Firfire Caverns
when you repeat
Floor 1
, so you may go back and choose the right option if you messed up the first time.
Talk to Alpha again.
Have at least 20 Fortitude, 20
Flamecharm
and 20
Thundercall
.
Return to surface and talk to
Delta
at
Miner's Landing
inside the lower-mountain part of the town, specifically behind the water mill located right where the waterfall is. In order to progress the Oath, you must kill
Chaser
or
Scion of Ethiron
For a more in-depth walkthrough, consider watching
this video
.
Arcwarder
cannot
be reobtained if you
Oathbreak
it.

or the

AND taking Klaris' light hook a total of 4 times (Killing the bosses does not grant progression, taking the light hook Klaris' spawns after killing them (not the one in your inventory) does. So killing chaser, going to floor 2, and using inventory light hook will not grant progression) granting one passive Talent/Mantra per kill until the Oath is fully progressed. Description
[
]
Oath
: Bladeharper
is one of the 17 obtainable Oaths in
Deepwoken
. It's known for the user's ability to gain extreme proficiency with its slash-based attacks, and the ability to massively buff its offensive means by landing consecutive hits or through sacrificing health.
“ Within your mind, many futures present themselves before you. Only your own must stand supreme. ”
― The Blade Within
The Bladeharper Oath is a result of the player conducting a
Reflection Ritual
at the Sky Statues scattered around the
Luminants
. The user makes a Vow with their own weapon, to spend the rest of their future with their own blade, out of the many futures presented to them during the Reflection Ritual.
Obtainment
[
]
The Oath requires 25 AGL or STR, plus either 75 points in Medium Weapon or a total of 90 points spread across any Weapon stats. The weapon equipped by a player must also not be
Fists
, else the interaction will not start the Oath quest.
Interact with one of the numerous
Sky Statues
located throughout both Luminants. This will transport you to Blade Within.
Defeat Mind's Reflection (a 1:1 copy of yourself, which has your currently equipped weapon, Talents and Mantras) to obtain the Oath.
It will use all Mantras you have, not just the ones you have equipped, and also Bladeharper Mantras that you haven't unlocked (e.g. On the first statue, the mirror can use Palm Strike and at the last statue the mirror can use Decimate). It's best to get rid of unwanted Mantras via
Deep Shrines
if you're not ready.
It seems to not have the same Mantra modifiers that you may have. For example: Frostdraw's Warden Blades can become bigger using stratus stones but the mirror will have the original size and possibly the original level (not tested).
The mirror starts out with 1 life in the first encounter, increasing by 1 for each encounter (1 + 2 + 3). When the mirror loses a life and still has lives, it vents then teleports back within the center of the Blade Within to return to neutral. This makes 6 in total to obtain and fully progress the oath.
NOTE:
Losing to your Bladeharper mirror
DOES
result in losing a life.
For a more in-depth walkthrough, consider watching
this video
.

Lower Erisia
Lower Erisia Statue Map
Sky Statue behind
Etris Palace
in
Etris
Etris Palace Statue Map
Sky Statue on the mountain peak near
Derelict Highchurch
Derelict Highchurch
Statue Map

can
be reobtained if you
Oathbreak
it.

[
]
The Oath can be progressed through interaction with the different other
Sky Statues
and defeating Mind's Reflection again with each, up to three times,

Description
[
]
Oath
: Blightsurger
is one of the 17 obtainable Oaths in
Deepwoken.
It is an Oath centered on the
Wither
Status and hindering your opponent. This Oath is associated with
The Hundred Legions
.
“ History only remembers the general, and never the soldier. [...] Together, we were an unstoppable force. ”
― Titus, Fulminus Destellus
Blightsurger draws most of its power from Sacred Lightning. It was invented by
Theros
, who passed it onto their disciples.
Titus
is one of its users and a trainer of the power, who used it alongside the 49th Legion - which he was the leader of - to conquer a majority of the Western Luminant in the name of The Empire.
Obtainment
[
]
To obtain Blightsurger, the player must defeat
Titus Fulminus Destellus
. Afterwards, approach Titus and select his second dialogue option. Finally, leave the
Merit Detainment Core
and speak to
Brutus
to obtain the Oath.
The player must have at least a sum of 80 points invested into Strength, Agility, or Fortitude, as well as a sum of 40 points invested into
Thundercall
or
Galebreathe
, and slightly higher than Ally reputation with
The Hundred Legions
to obtain Blightsurger.
Blightsurger
can
be reobtained if you
Oathbreak
it.
Progression
[
]
To progress Blightsurger, the player will need to activate
Sovereign State
multiple times
in combat
.
Recommended method is to hit training dummies. Attack until your
Blight meter
is full, then drop a note to be in combat. After that, activate Sovereign Mode and allow it to drain for 1 sec, then turn it off. It is not required to stay in the transformation the whole time or to attack in it.
10 ~ 100% Activations - Sovereign Drive
10 ~ 100% Activations - Blighted Touch
10 ~ 100% Activations - Skycrash/Blight Pierce
10 ~ 100% Activations - Warp Kick
10 ~ 100% Activations - Anchor Shock
10 ~ 100% Activations - Blight Lash
Rounded up in total would be 60 ~ 100
Sovereign Mode
activations.
You
cannot
oath skip Blightsurger. Attempting to do will have Brutus beat you down.

[
]

- Apply 70% of damage dealt as Wither.
Blightshock is applied through your Sovereign Bangle attacks and Blightsurge Mantras.
Blightshock from Sovereign Bangle deals 80% of its damage as Wither instead.
Blightshock can be further buffed with the Blight Pierce, Blighted Touch, and Anchor Shock Talents.

Description
[
]
Oath
: Blindseer
is one of the 17 obtainable Oaths in
Deepwoken.
The Oath is based around utility and uses sanity as its resource. It can serve many useful and potentially life-saving purposes in combat and in
The Depths
. It is the opposing Oath to
Visionshaper
.
Obtainment
[
]
Blindseer glyphs
at
the Temple of the Forgotten Flame
Meet the following requirements:
Have 40 Willpower
Have the 3 Talents from the
Mental Fortress
set:
Breathing Exercise
,
Conquer your Fears
and
Disbelief
from Talent rolls, not equipment. (You can reroll these into different talents at the
Shrine of Chance
once you obtain the oath)
Be blind through the
Blinded
Talent from Blindfolds.
Head to
Lower Erisia
and make your way to the village past the camp in the ruins.
Above Path's Respite lies the
Temple of the Forgotten Flame
. Climb up the wall bordering the village to reach it.
Climb above the temple doors or go through them if they are open and proceed inside the temple.
Watch out for the
Watcher
guarding the temple, as they will spawn a
Hollow Angel
if they catch sight of you. Once inside the temple, proceed through a passage on the left.
Inside the room, interact with the
Blindseer Glyph
while blind either from the Blind flaw or from a
Blindfold
(any other glyphs cannot be used to obtain the oath)
For a more in-depth walkthrough, consider watching
this video
.
Blindseer
can
be reobtained if you
Oathbreak
it.
Progression
[
]
To progress the Oath and obtain the Blindseer Mantras, you need to help Pathfinders, players who lack an Oath, in the
Depths Trial
. The first Mantra is unlocked after the first successful trial, the second at 3, and the last at 5. A passive Talent is also obtained after four trials (you can quickly leave three seconds after the Voice of Authority starts talking). Do note that helping people who have
Oathless
will not grant progression.
Blindseer will only progress if you are faced with a trial. If both the Blindseer and the Pathfinder are low enough level to not receive a trial, you will not gain Blindseer progression.
Alternatively, if you've fully progressed the Oath in a previous life, 10
Knowledge
can be spent to immediately unlock all related Talents.
The hints toward how to progress this Oath are glyphs located at
Su

Enchantments — Wiki - Deepwoken Builder
54
entries
Adhesive
Pulls your attacker towards you.
Allure
Incoming hits may Charm your attacker for 10s.
Astral
Grants +20% weapon damage in PvE. Call down stars alongside your critical attacks, and afer parrying thrice.
Blazing
Once enough stacks have been applied, for 5 seconds your attacks will ignite your foes.
Bluster
After landing a few stacks of Bluster, start a countdown to an explosion that scales off of your M1 damage. Land attacks during this countdown to make it explode faster. This explosion scales with…
Bounce
Incoming hits may bounce your attacker away.
Chilling
Chill your foes. Applies Bottom Freeze once enough stacks have been applied.
Curse of Captain Greene
Known throughout the Summer Company, many heard of the famous treasurer Captrain Greene. You inherit his strange ability of gaining damage the more rich you are. However since you aren't Captain…
Curse of Repulsion
When you block attacks, you knock your attacker backwards. You receive +30% chip damage.
Curse of the Authoritative
On critical, uppercut, or flourish, apply Authoritative on your opponent making them take 50% more posture damage. When you get guardbroken, take 50% more damage.
Curse of the Bloodthirsty
Deal +10% weapon damage and drain the blood of your foes, but suffer blood loss if your weapon attacks miss.
Curse of the No Life King
Grants passive regen and damages the sanity of your foes, but succumb to insanity much faster, lose twice as much blood, and never be more than a step away from death.
Curse of the Unbidden
Your critical attack is replaced with Unbidden Counter, restoring posture and stunning your attacker. Your attacks drain Ether on hit.
Curse of Void
Every enemy you slay while equipping this weapon will always be Drowned. Enemies hit will also experience reduced healing. It also applies 10 seconds of Ether Sunder to PvE enemies.
Curse of Ysley's Ember
Once enough stacks have been applied, the Fire of Life germinates. The explosion deals a random amount of damage and grants temporary health to the affected targets.
Curse of Yun'Shul
Has a chance to inflict a curse that deals percentage damage, but who it falls upon is often down to luck.
Deferred
Weapon damage is increased by 7%, but becomes damage over time, lasting for 2.5 seconds.
Detonation
Once enough stacks have been applied, an explosion is detonated.
Displacement
Incoming hits may proc Displacement, granting a speed boost and clearing all stuns present.
Drowned
Bolster the stats of your equipment, adding an additional +10 Health, +10 Ether, +5% Damage vs. Monsters, and +5% Monster Armor.
Elastic
Opponents are pulled towards you on hit.
Entanglement
Incoming hits may proc Entangled Defence, reducing your speed in exchange for 10% damage reduction.
Erebus
When landing a critical, make your opponent forget how to use a mantra for a minute.
Ferocity
Incoming hits may proc a speed boost and deal reduced damage. Both the speed boost and damage reduction scale with your current Tempo.
Gluttony
Steals Water and Stomach from those you hit.
Grim
Once enough stacks have been applied, Grim is applied for 6 seconds, increasing all incoming damage by 25% and all incoming posture damage by 50%.
Harrowing
Obscures your opponent's health, posture, and armor bars. Also causes PvE to get Confusion on successful hits.
Heroism
When above 75% HP, gain 4% damage for every 5% HP above 75%. When below 25% HP, gain 4% damage for every 5% HP below 25% HP. You can reflect negative status effects with light attacks or criticals to…
JOE's
It stands for Judicator's Original Enchantment. An enchant that was supposed to be only for the strongest of monster slayers. No one knows what happened to the original Judicator who made this…
Kyrsan Chill
On critical hits, apply a slow that increases with each crit. At 5 stacks fully encase your opponent in ice.
Life Weave
Unknown.
Metal
Steal Armor durability on hit.
Multiplicity
Incoming hits may grant you a random boon of Recovery, Healing, or Agility. 5s CD.
Nemesis
Your critical attack is replaced with Nemesis. Basic attacks now mark your enemies, allowing you to critical attack to teleport to them.
Nightbringer
Obfuscation
You gain some chip damage on m1 and crit.
Parasol's Blight
Hitting a player infects their body with Parasol's Blight. If a player who is infected is killed, their corpse is turned into one of the Parasol's Minions. This also has a chance of proccing on PvE…
Providence Thorns
Parrying attacks damages your foes.
Rain
Incoming hits may summon rain clouds above you.
Sear
Weapon gains 5% PEN. Applies Cauterize on hit for 15s, as well as draining their Water. Cauterized foes take more PEN the more stacks are applied.
Solar
Applies a Solar Mark on critical attack or flourish. Solar Marks last 8s and reduce Tempo gain and Ether regeneration. Your Vents drain the Tempo of those you've marked.
Stench
Incoming hits may produce a Stench Cloud, gradually draining 3% of their health if not blocked.
Stone
Do extra true damage whenever you guardbreak an opponent with a light attack or critical.
Storm
Shock your foes on hit every 5s. Rain will upgrade this shock into a lightning bolt called down from above.
Stormbreaker
Your critical attack is replaced with Stormbreaker Hurl, alloying you to launch your weapon. Shock your foes on hit every 5s. Occasionally, a lightning bolt may be called down from above.
Suffocated
Landing a critical, uppercut, flourish, or offhand attack procs suffocation.
Tainted Sorrow
Unknown.
Tears of the Edenkite
On critical attacks and flourishes, produce homing bubble projectiles. 20s CD.

Title: Flaws — Wiki - Deepwoken Builder
URL: https://deepwoken.co/wiki/flaw
Description: Character downsides.

==================================================

Flaws — Wiki - Deepwoken Builder
11
entries
Blind
You cannot see.
Deficient
You regenerate Ether more slowly.
Fugitive
Your past isn't so spotless. All Factions hate you.
Glutton
Your hunger and thirst is depleted faster.
Haemophilia
You bleed more.
Manic
All insanity gained from attacks is heavily amplified.
None
Obvious
Enemies detect you more easily.
Simple
You learn things at a slower rate than others.
Squeamish
Having to physically take the life of another makes you lose stomach.
Vegetarian
Eating the flesh of animals has a chance to make you vomit.

Title: Google Docs: Introduction
URL: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1Vc--1fU8IXyOn9zesPSijBK7W0J7Z-7FZbTx_-GFBFc/edit?tab=t.0
Description: Imported from Google Docs

==================================================

Introduction

Frostdraw





Info:

Difficulty To Play: ★★★☆☆

Versatility: ★★★★★

Rating For PvP: A+

Updated: June 12th, 2026

Made By: Rioshinki





About:

Frostdraw utilizes its own usage of mantras to apply its effects and pops off at creating opportunities to follow up and combo your opponents. The majority of the community has this attunement in any combo related build due to how versatile it is, with some investing 40 points into it even just for a single mantra. It’s seen in both comp and casual play as it's easy to learn, but hard to master completely as using one of mantras at the wrong time can lead to your opponent punishing you. 





Pros: Frostdraw is quick and has multiple mixups all of which are combo starters especially with the talent, Frozen Legs which prevents rolling. It also has tankiness as it gains resistance with the talent Orbital Ice. Frostdraw also gives the user access to tons of area control by placing ice on the floor which benefits them in various ways. There are benefits at both low and high investments and they can fit into almost any build. On top of this, it also has its uses for support builds as it contains multiple useful ranged mantras which are decent for third partying and applying its status effect.





Cons: Even though Frostdraw does contain tons of area control, it drastically lacks mobility. This is why it is typically seen more in chime than ganks as chime is an enclosed area. Frostdraw itself has no consistent mobility mantras and relies on other sources to keep up with other people. Frostdraw itself is lackluster in AOE mantras.





(Note: With the recent addition of Glacial Arc being destructible, Frostdraw’s mobility has never been worse. Ice Skate may help pass gaps in the Depths, but the main problem is how slow it is and it leaves you completely open to getting hit by ranged abilities for free.)





riowatermark









Point Optimization:





Low Frost: This can change depending on your build and what you’re using Frost for. You can opt for 40 Frostdraw pre-shrine if you just want Crystal Impale. Other than these, there's really no other benchmarks for low investment Frost.





Steam/Vaporborne: This path is reliant on both Ice and Flame mantras. It’s best to not just go 30 Frostdraw on this if you are able to.





Orbital Ice: Avoid only going 65 Frostdraw for this talent pre-shrine if needed as it scales down. While it’s good, you should only be trying to grab it on your 80 or high Frostdraw investment builds.





Traits: It is best to invest into Songchant over Proficiency on Frostdraw due to its high scaling, but there’s nothing wrong with just running 6 Vitality and 6 Erudition as well.





Misc: Frostdraw has no essential talents needed with the base stats that you shouldn’t have already on your build (Strength, Fortitude, Agility, etc.) So you don't have to worry about those revolving around Frostdraw.









Misc Info:





* Orbital “Oce” for those who know









Please don’t hesitate to @ me on Discord if you have any other questions relating to Frostdraw or building in general.

































Talent Selection

Talent Selection Guide









MUST TAKES/NEVER DROP:





Fragile Freeze: This talent allows you to follow up combos a lot easier when you guard break your opponent.





Saint Jay/Frostbite: You should ALWAYS be taking this, even on Crystal Path as you are still able to apply chilled. Saint Jay gives you more sustain throughout the fight.





Orbital Ice: Unless you did 65 Frostdraw pre (which you shouldn’t have), you will always take this talent. It is free resistances.





Cryonis: You should have multiple sources and ways of placing ice on the floor. This means you will always be getting use out of this talent.





Optional/Situational:





Unyielding Frost: Avoid getting this talent just for the sake of it. It's not worth the investment and is typically only taken on purpose for cheese strategies. If you are specifically going 100 Frostdraw for this talent, you should have a very good reason for it.





Preceding Chill: If you are using Ice Beam, you should always be taking this talent. It freezes your opponent for free allowing you or your teammates to follow up.





Frozen Legs: This talent is extremely build reliant as it only procs for 0.75 seconds. The way to use this talent is to movestack with a Frostdraw mantra such as Ice Cubes or Ice Forge first. This way, you can actually use another attack with this talent uptime. If you try using it with something like Ice Smash, adding in the hitstun and the windup animation of your next move, this will basically make the talent useless and impossible to follow up on properly. Drop this talent if you don’t have a way to realistically use it.





Frozen Pin-Cushion: If you are taking Ice Daggers, you should always be taking this talent. You will typically slide cast or be ready to follow up immediately after hitting this mantra. Freezing them allows you to do this easier.





Chilling Flourish: If you have Frost Wisp this talent is droppable. You don't need this talent since Wisp has this talent innate. If you don't though, you should always be taking this talent for the ice placement.





Cold Front: Venting places ice and instead of putting the situation at neutral, you'll slightly have an upper hand due to you being able to utilize the frost. You should always take this talent unless you have Ice Wisp where it becomes innate, meaning you can drop it otherwise.





Cool Head: This is an extremely good talent as it counters Flamecharm users. If you have the space you should grab it, but due to it relying on what your opponent is using which is inconsistent itself, this talent is droppable when tight on talents.





Glacial Coasting: If you’re using a Konga slide ring and/or you just slide cast consistently, there’s nothing wrong with taking this talent. More ice placement never hurts, but keep in mind that this talent is droppable.





Crystal Path: Crystalization / Reclaimed Glass / Crystal Shrapnel: When going Crystal, always take all of these talents. They all benefit you in some shape or form in all situations and builds.





Bottom Freeze: This talent pairs extremely well with mantras such as Ice Daggers, since it helps you follow up on your opponent easier while restraining their movement. This also makes them unable to turn, making backhitting with your mantras/m1s a lot easier. This talent is droppable if you don’t have any way to proc it, but if you do, you should always take it.





Frozen Anchor: Bottom Freeze is an extremely powerful status effect that completely stops your opponent from moving and turning. With this talent, you can essentially proc this talent every 10 seconds which is its cooldown. Keep in mind, this talent doesn’t have any drawback from shrining it down so don’t be afraid to do so. Even though this talent is a general “buff” to a Frostdraw build, try to only go for this talent if you are trying to confirm a move or make it easier to hit. An example of this can be Sightless Beam and/or Hailbreaker critical.





Stasis Strike: If you have a way to proc bottom freeze consistently and/or have Frozen Anchor, this is a decent talent to have. You’ll typically only be able to get this talent with Frozen Anchor and it’s not recommended to just stop at 90 of your weapon stat and 90 Frostdraw. Don’t go for this talent just for a bit of extra proc damage as the proc conditions are semi-inconsistent as you need to Bottom Freeze your opponent, then proc crystals.





Frost Buster: This talent is droppable but more ice placement never hurts to have. 





Glacial Mobility: You should never be getting Ice Blade in the first place due to how unviable it is currently, but if you do grab it for some reason, this makes the mantra a bit more bearable to use as it turns into one slash after a slide cast.





NEVER TAKE:





Condensation Drip: This talent only affects your passive thirst drain (which is easy enough to recover normally) so there’s no real reason to take this talent.





Misc/Hybrid:





Volcanic Glass: There’s no reason to not grab this talent if you are using the Eruption/Crystal hybrid combo. This makes it easier to proc eruptions meaning more DPS potential.





Temperature Shock: Make sure you grab this talent on your Eruption/Crystal hybrid builds. This makes it easier to proc crystals meaning more DPS potential.





Flashboil: On a normal Flamecharm/Frostdraw hybrid build, this talent is extremely powerful especially if you have some form of lockdown on your build. You are sacrificing the status effect of Chilling when proccing this, but with its recent nerfs, it’s not terrible to occasionally have a DPS boost from your Steam in a fight over Chilling.





Boiling Point: If you don’t have some consistent way to keep your opponent in the Steam for its DPS, Boiling Point is a perfect solution to do more damage instead of it just being useless aside from area control on the battlefield.





Fulgurite Formation: You should have a way to consistently proc either Crystal or Surge stacks on your build. An example of this would be using Boltcrusher as it applies surge stacks on m1 and it has a guardbreak critical which can instantly overload the opponent’s surge stacks causing Fulgurite Formation to proc. If you lack a way to get stacks on either or, it’s recommended to just take normal Frostdraw and Thundercall.





Freezing Wight: This talent allows you to proc Chill off of your Galebreathe mantras. If you are able to take this talent, you should. Avoid this talent if you are doing Low Galebreathe though as you’ll lack ways to proc Haunted Gale (and if you don’t I’m not sure why you’re taking multiple Gale Mantras on a low gale build).





Cold Blooded: This talent makes it extremely easy to stack up Blood Poison on your opponent which can proc Blood Poison related talents and its passives. Both normal Frostdraw and Crystals have the same potential as of how much Blood Poison they can stack, so just go the path that fits best with your current build.





Dual Attunements

Dual Attunement Ratings





(Key)





(★☆☆☆☆) - Unviable / Never Run

(★★☆☆☆) - Niche Synergy / Not Recommended

(★★★☆☆) - Viable / Some Weaknesses

(★★★★☆) - Powerful / Little To No Weaknesses

(★★★★★) - OP / No Weaknesses When Built Correctly









Flamecharm/Frostdraw (★★★★☆): This pair has two choices being Volcanic Glass or the Steam Path. Both offer insanely good DPS when used properly and both combo well into each other. The only real weakness of this dual attunement is the lack of overall chase/mobility.





Frostdraw/Thundercall (★★★★☆): With these two attunements you get access to Fulgurite Formation which is a status effect focused build with Crystal and Surge stacks. When utilized properly you can deal tons of burst damage and deal absurd amounts of damage. Normal Frostdraw and Thundercall is also a viable pick as well if you lack ways to stack Crystal and Surge stacks. Thundercall covers for Frostdraw’s major weakness of lacking gap closing tools and posture pressure. Frostdraw also provides excellent backhit mantras for Thundercall as well to capitalize off of shaky blocks. It’s highly recommended to go 80/80 on this pair and not go low investment on either especially on Fulgurite Formation.





Frostdraw/Galebreathe (★★★★★): Gale makes up for the lack of Frost’s mobility and also provides multiple mixups which fits into a builds overall kit. You can pick and choose your fights properly and be unstoppable in a gank or just be a chime menace with the amount of pressure you can apply to a single person.





Frostdraw/Shadowcast (★★★★☆): The amount of lockdown you have with this pair is extremely powerful. This is one of the most popular dual attunements as Shadow’s slow moves with Frost’s quick ones allow for excellent mixups and pressure against a single opponent. The only weakness of this pair is just overall mobility. 





Frostdraw/Ironsing (★★★★☆): The two tankiest attunements together in one. This pair makes you an unstoppable tank while still having crazy damage output. Ironsing can set up for Frost and Frost can set up for Ironsing so they go hand in hand perfectly together. The weakness of this pair is that Ironsing is typically extremely self reliant on its own usage so it doesn’t leave a lot of room for other oath mantras.





Frostdraw/Bloodrend (★★★★★): Cold Blooded is one of the most broken hybrids in the game currently. Hitting a couple of Frostdraw mantras will basically max out your opponent's Blood Poison which offers all sorts of debuffs towards them. Everyone you fight against will basically always be doing 12.5% less damage due to one of Blood Poisons passives as soon as you start fighting them. The only weakness of this pair is ways to set up for a guardbreak with Ice Smash consistently, but you still have plenty of ways to pressure posture. It’s highly recommended to go 80/80 on this pair (and even 85 Bloodrend for Gruesome Harvest!). Alternatively, you can also get away with low Bloodrend (40=<) and high Frostdraw. Avoid doing low Frostdraw and high Bloodrend.





Mantras

Mantra Selection Guide









IMPORTANT:





You will ALWAYS need some way of ice placement as you gain so many benefits from this. It is recommended you always take either Ice Wisp or Frozen Servants for consistency. There is ONE exception to this rule which is if you are using a legendary weapon that places ice for you, but even with this, it's still highly recommended you have some way to place ice consistently through your mantras. Keep this in mind when using Frostdraw.





S Tier Mantras:





Ice Lance: This mantra is literally Frostdraw’s signature move. You can use it to combo extend and it does an absurd amount of damage. It can also be used to gap close as it does have a lunge effect. You should always be taking this mantra on every Frostdraw build.





Ice Daggers: The animation on this mantra is so wacky it's almost impossible to read especially when slide casted. In laggier servers too, the daggers straight up just turn invisible as well. It also benefits from multiple talents which can be used for setting up combos. This is one of the best mantras for ice, but keep in mind it's not needed every single time. With its talent, Frozen-Pin Cushion, you freeze your opponent on hit.





Ice Smash: This is a true guard break at level 5 and procs Chill on-hit. It also has another hit that's optional afterwards so you can throw it out if your opponent dodges the first hit to attempt to roll catch.





Crystal Impale: This mantra is one of the best combo starters as when used properly it knocks your opponent down for a short duration if they were in the air allowing you to get a free hit in. It’s typically used after an uppercut due to its quick windup. You have to learn how to use this mantra properly though as you can’t really just throw it out randomly in a fight.





Glacial Arc: This mantra essentially a combat slot mantra on a mobility slot. While it may not seem very menacing, it does a crazy amount of damage while also having guardbreak potential due to its high posture damage. It’s highly recommended to take this mantra if you have some way to make up for your lack of mobility on Frostdraw or are simply just a chime only build.





A Tier Mantras:





Frozen Servants: This mantra is one of the best ice placement mantras in the game. When modified, this mantra can essentially cover a whole chime arena while still providing decent utility in a fight.





Ice Forge: When modified, this mantra can basically be impossible to parry because of the animation and hitboxes. Sometimes the parry timing is so off in laggier servers that you don’t get auto parry frames for the whole thing and it hits your block. You can use a Wayward Deep Gem on this to easily movestack something on top of this as well.





Frost Grab: This is an excellent roll catcher, applies chill which can set up for a combo, and is a popular pick among players. This mantra forces a parry trade with you having priority first.





Ice Carve: This mantra is especially good on Crystal as it procs tons of Crystals and is highly recommended to take on Crystal builds. You can throw this mantra out whenever and it doesn’t offer auto parry frames meaning they have to manually spam F when it comes out. Even when they do this, it still hits a couple times throughout the mantra.





Rising Frost: When slide casting this mantra, you can backhit and roll catch with it. This mantra also is extremely buggy and gains tons of Bloodless Gem healing on top of dealing insane damage.





Iceberg: This support mantra is a solid pick as it offers zone control while healing you. It forces your opponents to run into you which is good itself as well. Practice slide cast jumping this mantra moves you a bit and displaces you making it a bit harder for your opponent to try and cancel it as fast as possible.





Frost Wisp: This mantra is free ice placement around you every couple of seconds and should always be taken if you don't have another consistent source of ice placement. It also offers Cold Front and Chilling Flourish innately.





B Tier Mantras:





Ice Chain: This mantra can be clicked once for free pressure. If you hit it, you apply chill which can be used with Frozen Legs to movestack with something else. 





Ice Eruption: Be wary that you have to properly use this mantra as it has a huge amount of endlag essentially meaning you'll get punished if you miss it. This mantra cannot be parried or blocked, but they can dodge and also jump over it. It does allow you to hit a m1 or fast mantra afterwards, but doesn’t guarantee some slower mantras. Slide cast jumping will make it a lot easier to hit so practice doing this.





Ice Cubes: This is a good fill mantra to take if you have the space for it as the main point of taking this mantra is to movestack with it. If you use this mantra, practice slide casting it through your opponent so it hits a lot faster rather than just using it normally.





Ice Flock: You can movestack on top of this mantra and it deals a good amount of damage per hit. While not every hit is guaranteed, this also works in your favor as it’s harder for your opponent to parry it. 





C Tier Mantras:





Ice Beam: This provides good spacing especially if you make it comically large, but it isn’t necessarily the best pick over other ice mantras. If you do like this mantra though, there’s nothing wrong with taking it as it comes out fast and can be good for spacing.





Ice Fissure: This mantra is useful for applying Chill for free, zone control, and is sometimes taken on Frostdraw support builds. It's not the best pick, but there is essentially no counterplay to it besides not playing around it. Be aware that it uses tons of ether.





Crystal Knee: This mantra’s main point is literally just to set up for crystal impale. It’s recommended to only take this on Crystal builds as if you don’t have the Crystal Path the damage is nerfed heavily and you are better off just using an uppercut. 





NEVER TAKE:





Ice Spikes: Due to this mantra’s talent being vaulted, this mantra itself isn’t that good anymore. This mantra doesn’t get any extra scaling from levels and is purely reliant on just your Frostdraw investment. 





Warden’s Blades: This just gives your opponent’s free auto parry frames and screws you over when they know how to abuse them. 





Ice Blade: The main problem with this mantra is how easy it is to parry. It hits 4 times meaning realistically you’ll only get 1 or 2 hits in before they parry it. Even with its talent, Glacial Mobility, you are sacrificing half of its damage just to hit it meaning you would be better off just casting it normally as it’s a multihit which can at least roll catch at best.





Ice Lasers: This mantra is lackluster in DPS and there’s much better picks. Furthermore, it deals even less damage if you lack the Crystal Path.





Ice Skate: This mantra is just too slow and is easily punished. It also leaves you open to being hit by ranged mantras or projectiles and generally isn’t a good escape tool. Even if you use it in the water to get away faster, you leave a trail of ice that your opponents can still follow you on. Outside of combat, it’s great to have, but it offers you nothing whilst in combat besides some ice placement.





Weapons

Weapons





(Key)





(★☆☆☆☆) - Unviable / Never Run

(★★☆☆☆) - Niche Synergy / Not Recommended

(★★★☆☆) - Viable / Some Weaknesses

(★★★★☆) - Powerful / Little To No Weaknesses

(★★★★★) - OP / No Weaknesses When Built Correctly





Legendary Weapon Rating:





Hero Blade of Frost (★★★★★): This weapon is the best Hero Blade in the game currently. It has an extremely abusable critical attack that you can move stack with, places ice, and gives you area control on top of having an extremely short CD.





Gran Sudaraska (★★★★☆): This weapon places a considerable amount of ice, has decent DPS, an excellent critical, and is an extremely low investment. This being a heavy weapon allows for you to have even more mix ups and especially mix up uppercuts or other mantras.





Kyrswynter (★★★★☆): This weapon allows you to zone your opponent easily with its range and also has good DPS. It doesn’t allow you to have as much varying attack speeds for mixups, but it's still an incredibly balanced weapon and isn’t a bad pick.





Hailbreaker (★★★☆☆): This weapon has an extremely strong critical attack when utilized properly as you can slide cast it to hit your opponents a lot easier. It also procs either Chill or Crystals on every attack depending on your Frostdraw path. The only thing holding this weapon back is the high heavy investment needed to equip this, but is a powerful pick regardless.





Rimebreakers (★★★☆☆): There’s very little reason to take this weapon over Wraithclaw as Wraithclaw is 10 points less expensive if you were on a hybrid Frostdraw/Galebreathe build. Its innate talent is also not the best. You don’t want to sacrifice your mobility especially on Galebreathe, and Galebreathe is all about playing offensive and fast while this weapon tries to promote the opposite. The critical is alright, but it doesn’t apply stronger effects like Suffocate or proc a Apparition like Gale’s stronger legendary weapons.





Weapon Rating:





Light Weapons (★★★★☆): This is a good pair as Light Weapons and Frostdraw allow for you to apply insane pressure against an opponent, but this pair lacks decent gap closing. You also aren’t able to mix up uppercuts as well for other mantras like Crystal Impale.





Medium Weapons (★★★★★): This makes your build pretty balanced and allows for you to play passively or aggressively well. You also get provided with Medium’s arsenal of mantras such as Slice n’ Dice which can help with gap closing.





Heavy Weapons (★★★★☆): Heavy weapons are objectively the best with Frostdraw. This gives you posture pressure with your m1s and mix ups with your fast mantras. You should ALWAYS be taking Crystal Impale on a heavy Frostdraw build due to how abusable uppercuts are on Heavy as well.

Oaths

Oath Selection Rating





(Key)





(★☆☆☆☆) - Unviable / Never Run

(★★☆☆☆) - Niche Synergy / Not Recommended

(★★★☆☆) - Viable / Some Weaknesses

(★★★★☆) - Powerful / Little To No Weaknesses

(★★★★★) - OP / No Weaknesses When Built Correctly









Oathless (★★☆☆☆): Frostdraw does pop off of its own usage, but you should be looking at other oaths because Frostdraw helps set up for other things. You don't need the extra mantra slots to do this.





Blindseer (★★★☆☆): Frostdraw has many ways of setting up for Sightless Beam such as Crystal Impale. This allows for you to get a bunch of cheesy combos off that do 1 to 2 bars of damage with minimal effort. Having ice on the floor increases your sliding which can be used for abusing slide casting Sightless Beam.





Visionshaper (★★★☆☆): There is decent combo potential between Frostdraw and Visionshaper as both capitalize off of constant pressure and movestacks. The only real problem is you have no real gap closing tool with this pair.





Starkindred (★★★★★): This oath offers an uptilt which can be used with Crystal Impale or you can even mix it up by throwing a guardbreak. Frozen Legs can make landing Death From Above a lot easier or even backhitting Sinister Halo. Starkindred also innately provides some form of mobility which helps out Frostdraw as well.





Arcwarder (★★★★☆): This opens you up to one of the dual attunement paths like Volcanic Glass or Fulgurite Formation while providing mobility. The resistances from Frostdraw and the Arc Suit will just make you even tankier as well. This also gives you plenty of mantra slots to abuse the dual attunements as you'll have the mantra space for them. The only real weakness of this pair is stat points.





Linkstrider (★☆☆☆☆): Frostdraw contains some decent third party mantras but it’s not really the best at doing it. As a Linkstrider, you want to be slippery and have multiple forms of mobility which Frostdraw just lacks heavily in.





Jetstriker (★★★★★): Jetstriker goes hand in hand with Frostdraw’s high paced playstyle while covering for Frostdraw’s main weakness of lacking mobility. Jet Kick gap closes and can help start up combos if you hit it as well. 





Dawnwalker (★★☆☆☆): Frozen Legs can help with landing Radiant Dawn but offers no real gap closing for Frostdraw. 





Contractor (★★☆☆☆): Frostdraw realistically has no way to set up for Judgement and Contractor doesn’t have any form of gap closing as well.





Silentheart (☆☆☆☆☆): Yeah.





Chainwarden (★★★☆☆): Landing a chain can help out Frostdraw by giving it an easier time closing the gap due to mobility but the problem is landing the chain consistently. Frostdraw doesn’t have the best arsenal to bait out shaky block allowing you to land Restrain but the result of the chain is what helps out here. 





Bladeharper (★★★☆☆): Bladeharper pops off from uppercuts and one of Frostdraw’s combos is uppercut into Crystal Impale. Bladeharper also just fits well with Frostdraw’s high pressure making these two a scary pair in the hands of a capable player. Make sure you have some form of gap closing to properly utilize this pair.





Saintsworn (★★★☆☆): Majority of Frostdraw’s talents don’t work or are bugged with Saintsworn, but Frostdraw still offers its powerful arsenal of mantras meaning it can still fit well on majority of builds.





Soulbreaker (★★★☆☆): Soulbreaker can help with some mixups with Rhythm Advance but it doesn’t offer what Frostdraw needs to be successful.





Fadetrimmer (★★★★☆): Close Shave does offer some form of chase/mobility for Frostdraw to use so you can continue pressure on a person. Ice placement does also speed up your slide speed meaning slide casting Precision Cuts can be abused a lot easier as well.





Saltchemist (★★★☆☆): Frostdraw can gain a lot of area control and denial which does synchronize with Saltchemist’s goals as well. This is a powerful pair if you want to play a bit more passive and/or third party, but you’ll lack proper chase and gap closing.





Blightsurger (★★★★☆): Blightsurger is focused on spamming its mantras and knockdown. Frostdraw has multiple ways of adding on top of this fast paced oath and fits well with it on top of being able to further extend the knockdowns.

Adhesive

Allure

Astral

Blazing
Once enough stacks have been applied, for 5 seconds your attacks will ignite your foes.
Bluster
After landing a few stacks of Bluster, start a countdown to an explosion that scales off of your M1 damage. Land attacks during this countdown to make it explode faster. This explosion scales with…
Bounce
Incoming hits may bounce your attacker away.
Chilling
Chill your foes. Applies Bottom Freeze once enough stacks have been applied.
